//=========================================================================================================
// 名称: MModbEh.cpp
// 功能: 处理MModbus规约的流程控制
// 编写：刘秀鹏
// 改写：1.SOE配置合法性判断2.是否增加合法性判断同一寄存器地址，但ucDataType不一样和YX、YC、DD不一样
//=========================================================================================================
#include "prottask.h"

===== 切片 1 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
#if (MD_MMODBEH == YES)
#include "mmodbeh.h"

//=========================================================================================================
//  函数名称: MModbEh_Task                            
//  函数功能: 实现MModbus规约的应用层调度管理任务       
//  输入参数:                                          
//  返回值：无                                         
//=========================================================================================================
int  MModbEh_Task()
{

    CMModbEh *MModbEh=new CMModbEh();
	if(MModbEh->Init()!=TRUE)
		Exit();
    MModbEh->Run();
	return 1; 
}

//=========================================================================================================
//  函数名称: MModbus::Init         
//  函数功能: 实现MModbus规约的初始化 
//  输入参数:                         
//  返回值：无                        
//=========================================================================================================

{
	DWORD dwDevNo;
// ======== CommInfo 规约类型表填写规范 ========
		
	TPMI PMI;
	m_dwProtocolType=PT_MMODBEH;
	GetPMI(&PMI);						//获取主站类规约信息表
	PMI.dwGBAddress=0;		 			//广播报文的地址
	PMI.dwMaxErrCount=6;				//连续出错dwMaxErrCount次后通知数据库
	PMI.dwTxdRetryCount=3;				//重发数次
	PMI.dwMinRealDevNum=1;				//最少模块数
    PMI.dwTxdBufSize=MAX_TXDFM_SIZE;    //发送缓冲区大小
	PMI.dwRxdBufSize=MAX_RXDFM_SIZE;    //接收缓冲区大小
	PMI.dwMemBlockSize=MAX_RXDFM_SIZE;	//公用数据处理缓冲区大小,用pGetMemBlock()取得该缓冲区首址
	SetPMI(&PMI);       				//获取主站类规约信息表
	
	if(C_Master::Init() != TRUE)
		return FALSE;
	taskDelay(2000);
	
#ifdef PRINT_USERNAME
	strncpy(m_pUserName,PRINT_USERNAME,sizeof(m_pUserName));
#endif
	//分配内存
	m_pDevCfg = (TModbEhDevInfo *)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhDevInfo)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	m_pSOECfg =  (TModbEhSOEInfo *)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhSOEInfo)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	m_pFileCfg =  (TModbEhFileInfo *)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhFileInfo)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	
	m_pTimeCfg = (TModbEhTimeInfo *)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhTimeInfo)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	m_pRealParaCfg = (TModbEhParaExInfo *)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhParaExInfo)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);

	m_pPollRec = (TModbEhPollRec*)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhPollRec)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	m_pBOResetRec = (TModbEhBOResetRec*)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModbEhBOResetRec)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	m_Run = (TModehRun*)MemMalloc(sizeof(TModehRun)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);

    m_pRxdFm=(TBModbEhRxdFm*)m_Rxd.Buf;

	if(m_dwDevNum <= 5) 
	{
		m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle = ((m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle+49)/50)*50;//从收到报文到再次询问的间隔，防止过快
	}

===== 切片 5 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
if(m_ComCfg.wRespWaitTime <= m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle )
	{
		LOG(" ",MLE_WARNING,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"Read ModbusEh TaskPara set time interval error");
		m_ComCfg.wRespWaitTime = m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle + 1000;
	}

	if(m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle == 0)
	{
		 m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle = 200;
	}

	StartCommEvent(MCC_CommTxdIdle,m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle);	  //发送间隔
	StartCommEvent(MCC_RspTimeout,m_ComCfg.wRespWaitTime);	  //等待超时 避免接收启动发送和超时等待发送冲突，导致同时发送
  	StartCommEvent(MCC_ReadComm,MAX_RXDFM_SIZE/2);	//需要通道读消息
	StartDbaseEvent(MCC_BurstBI);//注册需要双点遥信变位消息
  	
	ClearPollRec();  //清接收记录，准备接收。
	ClearBOexeRec();
	SetAllSendFlag();
	SwitchToDevNo(0);	
	m_wNetcount = 0;
	m_wRequestSOECount=0;

    return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 7 =====

内容:
bool CMModbEh::Read_TaskCFGFile(void)

===== 切片 8 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
{
	char FileName[50];
	char *pFileBuf;         //整个文件读到此区
	WORD wFLen = 1024;
	WORD *pwProtoID;
	DWORD dwFileLen;
	WORD  wCrc16;
	
	TFileHeader *pFileHead;
	TModbEhTaskCFG *pTaskCFG;
		
	pFileBuf = (char *)MemMalloc(wFLen, MMEM_FILETEMP);
	if (pFileBuf ==  NULL)
		return FALSE;

	//获取任务级配置参数文件名
	if(GetTaskTableName(FileName) != true)
	{	
		MemFree(pFileBuf);//zzh for coverity
		return FALSE;
	}
	if(ReadFile(FileName, pFileBuf, wFLen, 0 ))
	{	
		MemFree(pFileBuf);		
		LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"Read ModbusEh TaskPara error");
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	pFileHead = (TFileHeader *)pFileBuf;
	pwProtoID = (WORD*)(pFileHead+1);
	pTaskCFG = (TModbEhTaskCFG *)(pwProtoID+1);
	
	if(m_ucBigEndian)
		SwapTaskPara(pFileHead,pwProtoID,pTaskCFG);
	
	dwFileLen = pFileHead->dwFileLen;
	wCrc16 = pFileHead->wCRC16;
	
	if(wCrc16 != PCrc((BYTE*)(pFileBuf+2),dwFileLen-2))
	{
		MemFree(pFileBuf);
		LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"ModbusEh TaskPara Crc Check Error");
		return FALSE;
	}	
	if(*pwProtoID != m_dwProtocolType)
	{
		MemFree(pFileBuf);	
		LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"ModbusEh ProtocolType Error");
		return FALSE;
	}

	/* 读取pCommonInfo中内容保存在m_ComCfg */
	m_ComCfg.ucTransMode = pTaskCFG->ucTransMode;
	m_ComCfg.wGBAddress = pTaskCFG->wGBAddress;
	m_ComCfg.ucCRCType = pTaskCFG->ucCRCType;
	m_ComCfg.ucTranSequence = pTaskCFG->ucTranSequence;

===== 切片 9 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle = pTaskCFG->wTxdCommIdle;
	m_ComCfg.wRespWaitTime  = pTaskCFG->wRespWaitTime;
	m_ComCfg.wTimeInterval  = pTaskCFG->wTimeInterval;
	m_ComCfg.wControlMode = pTaskCFG->wControlMode;

	printf("0=devnum=%d,commidle=%d,waitime=%d\n",m_dwDevNum,m_ComCfg.wRespWaitTime,m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle);
	MemFree(pFileBuf); 
	return TRUE;
}

{
	char FileName[50];
	char *pFileBuf;//整个文件读到此区
	DWORD dwTempFLen,dwFLen = 20*1024;
	DWORD dwFileLen;
	WORD  wCrc16;
	WORD *pwProtoID;
	
	DWORD dwDevNo;
	BYTE bSendNum;
	BYTE bTimeNum;
	BYTE bSoeByteNum;
	BYTE bSoeSendNum;
	BYTE bSoeTriggerNum;
	BYTE bFileTriggerNum;
	WORD wAINum = 0;
	WORD wSPINum = 0;
	WORD wCounterNum = 0;
	WORD wBONum = 0;
	WORD wAONum = 0;
	
	TFileHeader	     *pFileHead;
	TModbEhDevCFG    *pDevCFG;
	TModbEhSendCFG   *pSendCFG;
	TModbEhTimeCFG   *pTimeCFG;
	TModbEhRxdSOESetCFG *pRxdSoeCFG;
	TModbEhTxdSOESetCFG *pTxdSoeCFG;
	TRDevDataCatalog *pRealData;
	TModbEhAICFG     *pAICFG;
	TModbEhSPICFG    *pSPICFG;
	TModbEhDDCFG	 *pCounterCFG;
	TModbEhBOCFG 	 *pBOCFG;
	TModbEhAOCFG	 *pAOCFG;
	TModbEhSOETriggerCFG *pSOETrigger;
	TModbEhFileTriggerCFG *pFileTrigger;
	
	for(dwDevNo=0; dwDevNo<m_dwDevNum; dwDevNo++)
	{
		SwitchToDevNo(dwDevNo);
		::GetDevParaName(m_dwDevID, FileName);
		strcat(FileName, ".ptc");

		if(!GetFileLength(FileName, &dwTempFLen) )
			return FALSE;
		if(dwTempFLen > dwFLen)//保留最大文件大小
			dwFLen=dwTempFLen;
	}
	pFileBuf = (char *)MemMalloc(dwFLen, MMEM_FILETEMP);
	if( pFileBuf ==  NULL )
		return FALSE;

		SwitchToDevNo(dwDevNo);

		pFileTrigger = (TModbEhFileTriggerCFG *)(pSOETrigger + bSoeTriggerNum);
		if(m_ucBigEndian)	
			SwapDevicePara(pFileHead,pwProtoID,pDevCFG,pSendCFG,pTimeCFG,pRxdSoeCFG,pTxdSoeCFG,pSOETrigger,pFileTrigger);

			MemFree(pFileBuf);
			LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"MModbusEh DevPara too long Error");
			return FALSE;
		}

void CMModbEh::SwapRealParaNum(TRDevDataCatalog *pRealData)
{
	pRealData->wAITotal = SwapWord(pRealData->wAITotal);
	pRealData->wSPITotal = SwapWord(pRealData->wSPITotal);
	pRealData->wCounterTotal = SwapWord(pRealData->wCounterTotal);

void CMModbEh::SwapRealParaEx(WORD wAINum,TModbEhAICFG *pAICFG,WORD wSPINum,TModbEhSPICFG *pSPICFG,WORD wCounterNum,TModbEhDDCFG *pCounterCFG,WORD wBONum,TModbEhBOCFG *pBOCFG)
{
	int i;
	
	for(i=0;i<wAINum;i++)
	{
		pAICFG[i].wStartAddr = SwapWord(pAICFG[i].wStartAddr);
		pAICFG[i].fScale = SwapDWord(pAICFG[i].fScale);
	}
	for(i=0;i<wSPINum;i++)
	{
		pSPICFG[i].wStartAddr = SwapWord(pSPICFG[i].wStartAddr);
		pSPICFG[i].wRegisterBit = SwapWord(pSPICFG[i].wRegisterBit);
	}
	for(i=0;i<wCounterNum;i++)
	{
		pCounterCFG[i].wStartAddr = SwapWord(pCounterCFG[i].wStartAddr);
		pCounterCFG[i].fScale = SwapDWord(pCounterCFG[i].fScale);
	}
	for(i=0;i<wBONum;i++)
	{
		pBOCFG[i].wResetTime = SwapWord(pBOCFG[i].wResetTime);
	}
}

===== 切片 30 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能:检查SOE的参数设置是否合法
//  输入参数: 模块的序号
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE，失败
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Check_SOE_SetCfgPara(DWORD dwDevNo)
{
	int i;
	if(m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].wSOEType != SOEType_NoUsed)//有SOE，但是没有发送配置和接收内容配置
	{
		if(m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].ucSoePollIndex == 0 || m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum == 0)
		{
			LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"ModbusEx SOEpara ERR! Type Selected,but SOEConsentNum=0 and PollSOE set NULL!");
			return FALSE;
		}
		   
	}
	if((m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].wSOEType == SOEType_First_GetNum || \
		m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].wSOEType == SOEType_First_GetNumPtr || \
		m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].wSOEType == SOEType_First_GetNumCalcPtr))
	{
		for(i=0;i<m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].ucSoeSendNum;i++)
		{
			if(m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].pTxdSoeInfo[i].ucSendSOEType == Modbus_SendData_SOEContent)
			 	break;
		}
		if (i >= m_pSOECfg[dwDevNo].ucSoeSendNum)//未找到内容设置
		{
			LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"ModbusEx SOEpara ERR! The SOEtype need SOEConsent setting in SOETxdSetConfig!");
			return FALSE;
		}
			
	}
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 31 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::SetDevFileInfo(DWORD dwDevNo,TModbEhDevCFG *pDevCFG,TModbEhFileTriggerCFG *pTriggerCfg)
{
	int i;
	
	m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].ucFileTransType = pDevCFG->ucFileTransType;
	m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].ucTriggerNum = pDevCFG->ucFileTriggerNum;
	
	DWORD dwBINum=GetBINum(m_dwDevID);
	for(i=0;i<m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].ucTriggerNum;i++)
	{
		if(pTriggerCfg[i].ucDataType == Modbus_Trigger_Yx  && pTriggerCfg[i].wNo < dwBINum)
		{
			m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].pTriggerInfo[i].ucDataType = pTriggerCfg[i].ucDataType;
			m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].pTriggerInfo[i].wNo = pTriggerCfg[i].wNo;
			m_pFileCfg[dwDevNo].bNeedTrigger = TRUE;
		}
	}
	return TRUE;
}

			m_pRealParaCfg[dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[i].ucSelectNum = result;
			result = Check_ControlCent(pBOCFG[i].OperateCent,m_pRealParaCfg[dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[i].ucOperateCent);
			if(result <=0)//执行不能没有有效内容串
			{
				LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"BO Operate Config Set Err, Errcode=%d",result);
				return FALSE;
			}
			m_pRealParaCfg[dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[i].ucOperateNum = result;
			result = Check_ControlCent(pBOCFG[i].CancelCent,m_pRealParaCfg[dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[i].ucCancelCent);
			if(result < 0)
			{
				LOG(" ",MLE_ERROR,(((DWORD)m_wPortType)<<16)|m_wPortNo,"BO Cancel Config Set Err, Errcode=%d",result);
				return FALSE;
			}
			m_pRealParaCfg[dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[i].ucCancelNum = result;	
		}

===== 切片 42 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
int CMModbEh::Check_ControlCent(char *src, BYTE *dsc)
{
	int i=0,j=0,m=0;
	int len;
	BYTE consent[CONTRONL_MES_LEN] = "\0";

	len = strlen(src);
	
	if(len > CONTRONL_MES_LEN)
		return -3;//报文长度超限制
	if(len == 1 && src[0] == '0')//兼容部分版本工具，未配置遥控、遥调内容是默认为1个字符‘0’的情况
		return 0;
	for(i=0,j=0;i<len && m<CONTRONL_MES_LEN;i++)
	{
		if(src[i] ==' ')//空格继续
			continue;
		else if(src[i]>='a'&&src[i]<='f')
			consent[j]= src[i] - 'a' + 10;
		else if(src[i]>='A'&&src[i]<='F')
			consent[j]= src[i] - 'A' + 10;
		else if(src[i]>='0'&&src[i]<='9')
			consent[j]= src[i] - '0';
		else
			return -1;//字符非法
		j++;
		if(j%2 == 0)
		{	
			dsc[m] = consent[j-2]*16+consent[j-1];
			m++;
		}
	}
	if(j%2 == 1)
		return -2;//字符长度错误
	return m;
}

void CMModbEh::SetAllSendFlag()
{
	TSendFunInfo *pTxdFuncInfo;
	
	for(DWORD dwDevNo=0; dwDevNo<m_dwDevNum; dwDevNo++)
	{
		if(m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].ucTimeAble == 1)
			SetSFlag(dwDevNo,SF_SetTime);
		
		BYTE bTxdNum = m_pDevCfg[dwDevNo].ucSendNum;//该模块设置的功能码个数,发送时每个功能码相当于1组数据,请求数据时以应答帧长不超过255设置请求数量
		for(WORD wNo = 0 ; wNo<bTxdNum; wNo++)
		{
			pTxdFuncInfo = &m_pDevCfg[dwDevNo].pSendFunInfo[wNo];
			pTxdFuncInfo->wSendFlag = MODBIT_0;
		}
	}
}

//判断系统是否为大端
bool CMModbEh::IsBigEndian(void)
{
	union TWINBYTE val;

	val.w = 0xccaa;
	if(val.b.hi == 0xaa) 
		return TRUE;
	else 
		return FALSE;
}
bool CMModbEh::SwapFloatValue(BYTE *pfvalue,BYTE num)
{
	BYTE Temp[16];
	
	if(num>16)
		return FALSE;
	memcpy(Temp,pfvalue,num);
	if(m_ucBigEndian)
	{
		for(int i=0;i<num;i++)	
			pfvalue[i] = Temp[num-1-i];		
	}
	return TRUE;
}
DWORD CMModbEh::GetBINO_FromInfAddr( DWORD dwDevID,DWORD dwInfAddr)
{
	DWORD dwBINo;
	
	if(dwDevID >= gVars.dwDevNum)
		return 0xFFFFFFFF;

	if(NULL == gpDevice[dwDevID].pRealBase)
		return 0xFFFFFFFF;

	TRealBI *pRealBI=gpDevice[dwDevID].pRealBase->pRealBI;
	if(pRealBI==NULL)
		return 0xFFFFFFFF;
	
	if(gpDevice[dwDevID].pBI == NULL || gpDevice[dwDevID].pBIStatus == NULL)
		return 0xFFFFFFFF;

	for(dwBINo = 0;dwBINo < gpDevice[dwDevID].dwBINum;dwBINo ++)
	{
		if(dwInfAddr == pRealBI[dwBINo].dwObjAddr)
			break;
	}
	if(dwBINo >= gpDevice[dwDevID].dwBINum)
		return 0xFFFFFFFF;
	return dwBINo;
}

===== 切片 47 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
DWORD CMModbEh::DostoUnix(TSysTime *pTime,WORD wStartYear)
{
	BYTE MonthDays[12]={31,28,31,30,31,30,31,31,30,31,30,31};
	BYTE LeapMonthDays[12]={31,29,31,30,31,30,31,31,30,31,30,31};
	DWORD Month[12]={MaxMonth,MinMonth,MaxMonth,MinMonth,MaxMonth,MinMonth,MaxMonth,MaxMonth,MinMonth,MaxMonth,MinMonth,MaxMonth};
	DWORD i=0,t;

	if(!ClockInvalidate(pTime) || wStartYear > pTime->Year)
		return 0;

    if(pTime->Year%4==0 && pTime->Year%100!=0 || pTime->Year%400==0)
        Month[1] = Leap2Month;
    else
        Month[1] = NoneLeap2Month;

    for(t = wStartYear;t < pTime->Year;t++)
    {
        if((t%4==0 && t%100!=0) || (t%400==0))
            i += LeapYearSecond;
        else
            i += NoneLeapYearSecond;
    }

    for(t = 0;t < pTime->Month-1;t++)
        i += Month[t];       //24*60;

    i += (pTime->Day - 1)*86400; //24*60*60;
    i += pTime->Hour*3600;     //60;
    i += pTime->Minute*60;
    i += pTime->Second;

    return i;
}

WORD CMModbEh::CRC16MODBUS(BYTE *Buf,WORD len)
{
	WORD Rc,i,j;
 
	Rc=0xffff;
	for(i=0;i<len;i++)
   {
		Rc^=Buf[i];
		for(j=0;j<8;j++)
		{
			if(Rc&1) 
			{
				Rc>>=1; 
				Rc^=0xa001;
			}
			else 
				Rc>>=1;
		}
   }
	return Rc;
}

===== 切片 48 =====
来源: mmodbeh.cpp
内容:
WORD CMModbEh::CRC16XMODEM(BYTE* pBuf, int nLen)
{
 	WORD crc, i;

	crc = 0;
	while ((nLen--)>0) //循环次数等于count
	{
		crc = (crc ^ (((int)*pBuf++) << 8));
		for (i = 0; i < 8; ++i)//循环次数等于8
		{
			if (crc & 0x8000)
				crc = ((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021);
			else
				crc = crc << 1;
		}	
	}
	return (crc & 0xFFFF);
}

bool CMModbEh::InitOther()	
{
	bool rc;

	{
		rc = InitSoe();
		if(!rc)
			return FALSE;
	}
	if(m_pFileCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucFileTransType != FileMode_NoUsed)
	{
		rc = InitFile();
		if(!rc)
			return FALSE;
	}
	return TRUE;	
}

bool CMModbEh::InitFile()	
{
	
	 switch(m_pFileCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucFileTransType)
	 {
		 case FileMode_SND_MTZ:
	 		return InitSNDMTZFile();
	 }

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 初始化SOE、录波文件等其它功能所使用的变量内存等
//  输入参数: 
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::InitSNDMTZFile()	
{
	m_pSNDFile = (TSNDMTZFile*)MemMalloc(sizeof(TSNDMTZFile)*m_dwDevNum, MMEM_MMODBUSINFO);
	if(m_pSNDFile == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	memset(m_pSNDFile,0,sizeof(TSNDMTZFile)*m_dwDevNum);

	char sDevName[20];

	for(DWORD dwDevNo=0; dwDevNo<m_dwDevNum; dwDevNo++)
	{
		SwitchToDevNo(dwDevNo);
		m_pSNDFile[dwDevNo].wContinueMax = 900/(m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle+30);
		if(m_pSNDFile[dwDevNo].wContinueMax < 10)
			m_pSNDFile[dwDevNo].wContinueMax = 10;
		if(!(::GetDevParaName(m_dwDevID,sDevName)))
			return FALSE;
		m_pSNDFile[dwDevNo].ucLastSerial = GetConfigItemInt("SNDMTZ.ini", sDevName, "WFCSerial", 0);
		printf("%s file seq=%d\n",sDevName,m_pSNDFile[dwDevNo].ucLastSerial);
	}
	return TRUE;
}

    bool 		(*Init)(void);         	/*初始化入口*/
    bool 		(*Txd)(void);         	/*发送入口*/
    bool 		(*Rxd)(void);         	/*接收入口*/
    bool 		(*TimeOut)(void);       /*定时入口*/
}PACKED;

#if (MD_MMODBEH == YES)
#include "mmodbeh.h"

extern void ResetComm(DWORD dwPortID);
extern DWORD GetRealYkNo(DWORD dwDevID, DWORD dwBOID, DWORD dwBOAddr);
//=========================================================================================================
//  函数功能: 定时任务的处理
//=========================================================================================================
void CMModbEh::OnTimeOut(DWORD dwTimerID)
{

	C_Master::OnTimeOut(dwTimerID);

	switch(dwTimerID)
	{
		case TM_BORESET:
			if(SwitchClearSFlag(SF_BOSetTime))
			{
				SetSFlag(SF_BORESET);	//遥控复归置位
				KillTimer(TM_BORESET);
				return ;
			}
			break;
		case TM_1S:
		{
			DWORD dwSaveDevNo = m_dwDevNo;
			SetTxdQueryFlag();//轮询周期使能
			SetTimeFlag();//对时标志设置
			CheckTrigger();
			break;
		}
	}
	
    return;       
}

bool CMModbEh::OnBOSet(DWORD dwAppID,DWORD dwDevID,DWORD dwCommand,DWORD dwBONo,DWORD dwAttrib,DWORD dwRetNo,
							DWORD dwRetDevID,DWORD dwDegree,DWORD dwTimeH,DWORD dwTimeL,DWORD dwStatus)//遥控操作

===== 切片 87 =====
来源: mmodbehm.cpp
内容:
{
	
	if(C_Master::OnBOSet(dwAppID,dwDevID,dwCommand,dwBONo,dwAttrib,dwRetNo,dwRetDevID,dwDegree,dwTimeH,dwTimeL,dwStatus)!=TRUE)//遥控操作
		return FALSE;
	
	//通讯中断时直接拒绝
	if(GetSFlag(SF_DevErr))
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_HaveBO);
		SetBOReturn(dwBONo, PBORET_FAIL);
		return TRUE;
	}
	
	DWORD dwBOIndex;
	TBOParaExInfo  TBOParaExInfo;//遥控内容
	
	TBO* pBO=pGetBO();
	if(pBO == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	dwBOIndex = GetRealYkNo(m_dwDevID, pBO->dwNo, pBO->dwAttrib);
	if(dwBOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wBONum)
	{
		SetBOReturn(pBO->dwNo,PBORET_FAIL);
		ClearSFlag(SF_HaveBO);
		return FALSE;
	}
	TBOParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[dwBOIndex];
	switch(pBO->dwCommand)
	{
		case TP_YKSELECT:
			if(TBOParaExInfo.ucSelectNum == 0)//没有设置
			{
				SetBOReturn(pBO->dwNo,PBORET_SUCCESS);
				ClearSFlag(SF_HaveBO);
			}
			break;
		case TP_YKOPERATE:
		case TP_YKDIRECT:
			if(TBOParaExInfo.ucOperateNum == 0)//没有设置
			{
			    SetBOReturn(pBO->dwNo,PBORET_FAIL);
				ClearSFlag(SF_HaveBO);
			}	
			break;
		case TP_YKCANCEL:
			if(TBOParaExInfo.ucCancelNum == 0)//没有设置
			{
				SetBOReturn(pBO->dwNo,PBORET_SUCCESS);
				ClearSFlag(SF_HaveBO);
			}
			break;
		default:
			break;
	}
	if(GetSFlag(SF_HaveBO))
		m_pTimeOut[m_dwDevNo].wBOTimeOutCount = BOTIMEOUTMAX;
	return TRUE;	
}

===== 切片 88 =====
来源: mmodbehm.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::OnAOSet(DWORD dwAppID,DWORD dwDevID,DWORD dwCommand,DWORD dwAONo,DWORD AOValue,DWORD dwRTNNo,
							DWORD dwRetDevID,DWORD sRetValue,DWORD dwTimeH,DWORD dwTimeL,DWORD dwStatus)
{
	if(C_Master::OnAOSet(dwAppID,dwDevID,dwCommand,dwAONo,AOValue,dwRTNNo,dwRetDevID,sRetValue,dwTimeH,dwTimeL,dwStatus)!=TRUE)
		return FALSE;

	//通讯中断时直接拒绝
	if(GetSFlag(SF_DevErr))
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_HaveAO);
		SetAOReturn(PAORET_FAIL);
		return TRUE;
	}

	DWORD dwAOIndex;
	TAOParaExInfo  TAOParaExInfo;//遥调内容
	TAO* pAO=pGetAO();
	if(pAO == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	dwAOIndex=GetAONo(m_dwDevID, pAO->wNo);
	if(dwAOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wAONum)
	{
		SetAOReturn(pAO->wNo,PBORET_FAIL);
		ClearSFlag(SF_HaveAO);
		return FALSE;
	}
	TAOParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTAOParaExInfo[dwAOIndex];
	switch(pAO->dwCommand)
	{
		case TP_AOSELECT:
			if(TAOParaExInfo.ucSelectNum == 0)//没有设置
			{
				SetAOReturn(pAO->wNo,PBORET_SUCCESS);
				ClearSFlag(SF_HaveAO);
			}
			break;
		case TP_AOOPERATE:
		case TP_AODIRECT:
		    if(TAOParaExInfo.ucOperateNum == 0)//没有设置
			{
				SetAOReturn(pAO->wNo,PBORET_FAIL);
				ClearSFlag(SF_HaveAO);
			}
			break;
		default:
			break;
	}
	if(GetSFlag(SF_HaveAO))
		m_pTimeOut[m_dwDevNo].wAOTimeOutCount = AOtimeOutMax;
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 89 =====
来源: mmodbehm.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::SetTxdQueryFlag()
{
	DWORD dwDevNo;
	BYTE bTxdNum;
	WORD wNo;
	TSendFunInfo *pTxdFuncInfo;
		
	for(dwDevNo=0; dwDevNo<m_dwDevNum; dwDevNo++)
	{
		bTxdNum = m_pDevCfg[dwDevNo].ucSendNum;//该模块设置的功能码个数,发送时每个功能码相当于1组数据,请求数据时以应答帧长不超过255设置请求数量
		for(wNo = 0 ; wNo<bTxdNum; wNo++)
		{
			pTxdFuncInfo = &m_pDevCfg[dwDevNo].pSendFunInfo[wNo];
			if(pTxdFuncInfo->wSendCount > 0)
			{
				pTxdFuncInfo->wSendCount --;
				if(pTxdFuncInfo->wSendCount <=0)
					pTxdFuncInfo->wSendFlag |= MODBIT_0;
			}
		}
	}

	return TRUE;	
}
bool CMModbEh::SetTimeFlag()
{
	DWORD dwDevNo;
		
	for(dwDevNo=0; dwDevNo<m_dwDevNum; dwDevNo++)
	{
		if(m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].ucTimeAble == 1 && m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].wTmCount >0)
		{
			m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].wTmCount --;
			if(m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].wTmCount == 0)
			{
				printf("\nm_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].wTmCount =%d\n",m_pTimeCfg[dwDevNo].wTmCount);
				SetSFlag(dwDevNo,SF_SetTime);
			}
		}
	}
	return TRUE;	
}

bool CMModbEh::IsSpecialTimeSyncCtrlBO(void)
{
	TBO* pBO = pGetBO();
	if (pBO == NULL)
		return FALSE;

	return (pBO->dwNo == MODBEH_SPECIAL_TIME_SYNC_BOADDR) ||
			(pBO->dwAttrib == MODBEH_SPECIAL_TIME_SYNC_BOADDR);
}

#if (MD_MMODBEH == YES)
#include "mmodbeh.h"
extern void sMsSleep ( signed long int ms);
//=========================================================================================================
// 名称: 
// 功能: 处理MModbEh规约的接收
// 编写：
// 改写：
//  返回值：  无                        
//=========================================================================================================
bool CMModbEh::RxdMonitor(void)
{
	PBYTE pData;
	WORD wRFunCodeNum;
	int i;
	
    if(SearchFrame()!=TRUE)	
		return FALSE;//没找到有效报文
			
	switch(m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wDatatype)	//根据接收来判定怎样接收。
	{
		case Modbus_DataType_BASE:
		case Modbus_DataType_YC:
		case Modbus_DataType_SPI:
		case Modbus_DataType_DD:
			Rxd_BaseData(m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wDatatype);
			break;
		case Modbus_DataType_BO:
			Rxd_BOData();
			break;
		case Modbus_DataType_AO:
			Rxd_AOData();
			break;
		case Modbus_DataType_CALLSOE:
			Rxd_SOEReply();
			break;
		case Modbus_DataType_CALLFILE:
			Rxd_FileReply();
			break;
		default:			
			break;
	}

	//if(m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle != 0 && m_dwDevNum <=5)//模块多时不需要增加延时,利用多个模块依次轮询的正常的时间为间隔
	//	sMsSleep(m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle);
    
	//TxdMonitor();   //delete by lyp 2025/11 在底层增加超时等待，并在底层增加了接受ok后启动发送机制
	return TRUE;
 
}

//=========================================================================================================
//  函数功能: 检查Buf处是否是一个有效报文
//  输入参数: Buf:要检查的报文起始地址

//  返回值：  
//           FM_OK  +实际报文长度   //检测到一个完整的帧

===== 切片 99 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//           FM_ERR +已处理字节数   //检测到一个校验错误的帧
//           FM_LESS+已处理字节数   //检测到一个不完整的帧（还未收齐）
//  注:已处理字节数若为0则下次搜索从下一地址开始，否则下次搜索将跳过已处理过的字节，从而加快搜索速度
//=========================================================================================================

{
	WORD wMBAPFrameLen=0;//MODBUS-TCP在MBAP头中的报文长度
	char sMsg[100];
	WORD wFrameLen=0;
	BYTE bDifflen=MODBUS_CRCTAIL_SIZE;
	PBYTE pData;
	WORD wEvenCount;
	if(Len<5)
		return FM_LESS; 
	
	if(m_ComCfg.wControlMode&MODBIT_0)//SM4加解密
	{
		if(Len%16 != 0)//长度为16的倍数
			return FM_LESS;
		SM4DecryptRxdFM(Buf,Len);
	}
	
	pData = Buf;
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTransMode == TransMode_Network) //以太网，没有校验码
	{
		if(Len<9)// 功能码1/2/3/4到数据长度
			return FM_LESS;
		
		//TCP报文，判断事务处理标识(序列号)
		wEvenCount = MAKEWORD(pData[1],pData[0]);
		if(m_ComCfg.wControlMode&MODBIT_1){
			if(wEvenCount != m_wNetcount )
			{
				snprintf(sMsg,sizeof(sMsg)," The Net Mopudbus RxdFM netcount is err!");
            	if(gVars.CommBlock.dwPortID==m_dwPortID)
		   	 	{
					WritePrompt("",sMsg);		
		   	 	}
				return FM_ERR|Len;
			}
		}

		bDifflen = MODBUS_NETHEAD_SIZE;//net头
		wMBAPFrameLen = pData[5] + MODBUS_NETHEAD_SIZE;
		pData +=MODBUS_NETHEAD_SIZE;  //指向从站地址
	}
	m_pRxdFm = (TBModbEhRxdFm *)pData;
	
	if(SwitchToAddress(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucAddress)!= TRUE)
	{
		snprintf(sMsg,sizeof(sMsg),"Address not found,Addr=%d",m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucAddress);
		if(gVars.CommBlock.dwPortID==m_dwPortID)
		{
			WritePrompt((char *)"",sMsg);
		}

		return FM_ERR|Len;
	}

===== 切片 102 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp

if( (m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc&0x7f) != m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].ucFunCode) //接收与发送的功能码不一致
	{
		snprintf(sMsg,sizeof(sMsg),"funcode isn't Txd,rxd=%d,txd=%d",m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc,m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].ucFunCode);
		if(gVars.CommBlock.dwPortID==m_dwPortID)
		{
			WritePrompt((char *)"",sMsg);
		}

		return FM_ERR|Len; 
	}
	if(!CheckFrameLen(&wFrameLen,wMBAPFrameLen,bDifflen))
		return FM_ERR|Len;
		
	if(Len < wFrameLen)
		return FM_LESS;
	
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTransMode == TransMode_Serial) //串口CRC校验
	{
		//CRC校验
		if(!CheckFrameCRC(Buf,wFrameLen))
			return FM_ERR|Len;
	}
	
	return FM_OK|wFrameLen;
	
}

		case 1:
		case 2:

		case 15:
		case 16:

				wFrameLen = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wTxdFmLen;//写镜像回复
			else
				wFrameLen = 6 + bDifflen;

			break;

					wFrameLen = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wTxdFmLen;
				else
					wFrameLen= 3 + bDifflen;
				wExpectLen = wFrameLen;
			}else
				return FALSE;
	}

	*pwFrameLen = wFrameLen;
	
	//netFrame
	if(wMBAPFrameLen > 0 && wMBAPFrameLen != wFrameLen)
	{
		sprintf(sMsg,"netframe len isn't expected,rxd=%d,expected=%d,wMBAPLen=%d",wFrameLen,wExpectLen,wMBAPFrameLen);
		if(gVars.CommBlock.dwPortID==m_dwPortID)
		{
			WritePrompt((char *)"",sMsg);
		}
		return FALSE;
	}
	//不需要检查

===== 切片 106 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::CheckFrameCRC(PBYTE Buf,WORD wFrameLen)
{
	char sMsg[100];
	WORD wRc;   
	WORD wCrc;

	switch(m_ComCfg.ucCRCType)
	{
		case CRC16_MODBUS:
			wRc = CRC16MODBUS(Buf,wFrameLen-2);
			break;
		case CRC16_XMODEM:
			wRc = CRC16XMODEM(Buf,wFrameLen-2);
			break;
		default:
			wRc = CRC16MODBUS(Buf,wFrameLen-2);//默认
			break;
	}
		//高低字节顺序
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTranSequence == FirstLOBYTE ) 
		wCrc = MAKEWORD(Buf[wFrameLen-2], Buf[wFrameLen-1]);
	else
		wCrc = MAKEWORD(Buf[wFrameLen-1], Buf[wFrameLen-2]);
		
	if( wCrc!=wRc)		//校验错误
	{
		sprintf(sMsg,"CRC error,rxd=%04X,cal=%04X",wCrc,wRc);
		if(gVars.CommBlock.dwPortID==m_dwPortID)
		{
			WritePrompt((char *)"",sMsg);
		}
		return FALSE;
	}
	return TRUE;
}
void CMModbEh::SM4DecryptRxdFM(PBYTE Buf,int Len)
{
	int i;
	sm4_context ctx,ctx1;
	BYTE  key[16]={0x01,0x02,0x03,0x04,0x05,0x06,0x07,0x08,0x09,0x10,0x11,0x12,0x13,0x14,0x15,0x16};
	BYTE  iv[16]={0};
	
	sm4_setkey_dec(&ctx, key);
	printf("==================================================================\n");		
	for(i=0;i<Len;i++)
		printf("%02x ", Buf[i]);
	printf("\n");

	sm4_crypt_cbc(&ctx, SM4_DECRYPT, Len, iv, Buf, Buf);
	for(i=0;i<Len;i++)
		printf("%02x ", Buf[i]);
	printf("\n==================================================================\n");
	return;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: sMsSleep
//  函数功能: 毫秒休眠
//  输入参数: ms:毫秒数
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;

===== 切片 107 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
void CMModbEh::sMsSleep (long ms)
{
	struct timespec rqtp;

	rqtp.tv_sec = ms / 1000;
	rqtp.tv_nsec =  (ms % 1000) * 1000000;    //纳秒

	nanosleep (&rqtp, NULL);	/* should be available on AIX v5.x	*/
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_BaseData
//  函数功能: 实现遥信、遥测、电度的接收处理
//  输入参数:
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 108 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_BaseData(WORD datatype)

===== 切片 109 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
{
	BYTE bFunCode;
	BYTE bBitRegFlag = 0;
	BYTE bByteNum;
	WORD wStartAddr;
	WORD wEndAddr;
	WORD wIndex = 0;
	WORD wInfoNo;
	WORD wSortNum;
	
	TModbEhDevParaSort *pDevSortPara;//寄存器地址排序
	
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	bFunCode = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc&0x7f;
	if(bFunCode == 1 || bFunCode == 2)//位寄存器，只为遥信
		bBitRegFlag = 1;
	
	bByteNum = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount;	
	wStartAddr = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wStartAddr;
	if(bBitRegFlag)
		wEndAddr = wStartAddr + bByteNum*8;
	else
		wEndAddr = wStartAddr + (bByteNum+1)/2;//后面处理，注意补零。
		
	wSortNum = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wSortNum;
	pDevSortPara = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pDevSortInfo;

===== 切片 110 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
for(wIndex = 0;wIndex < wSortNum;wIndex++)
	{
		if(pDevSortPara[wIndex].wStartAddr >= wStartAddr&&pDevSortPara[wIndex].wStartAddr <=wEndAddr)
		{
			if(bBitRegFlag == 1&&pDevSortPara[wIndex].ucParaType !=Modbus_DataType_SPI)//位寄存器，只解析遥信
				continue;
			if(datatype != Modbus_DataType_BASE && datatype != pDevSortPara[wIndex].ucParaType)//不是混合类型，同时接收的类型和装置类型不相同
				continue;
			wInfoNo = pDevSortPara[wIndex].wNo;
			switch(pDevSortPara[wIndex].ucParaType)	//根据接收来判定怎样接收。
			{
				case Modbus_DataType_YC:
					if(wInfoNo < m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wAIINum)
						Rxd_Yc(wInfoNo);
					break;
				case Modbus_DataType_SPI:
					if(wInfoNo < m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wSPINum)
					{
						//功能码校验:点表配置了数据组功能码(非0)时,仅处理与应答帧功能码一致的数据组,
						//防止不同功能码数据组寄存器地址重叠时,遥信被其他数据组报文刷新导致跳变
						if(m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTSPIParaExInfo[wInfoNo].ucFunCode != 0
							&& m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTSPIParaExInfo[wInfoNo].ucFunCode != bFunCode)
							break;
						Rxd_Yx(wInfoNo,bBitRegFlag);
					}
					break;
				case Modbus_DataType_DD:
					if(wInfoNo < m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wDDNum)
						Rxd_DD(wInfoNo);
					break;
				default:
					return FALSE;
			}
			
		}
		if(pDevSortPara[wIndex].wStartAddr > wEndAddr)//起始地址排序，后面的无需再判断,退出循环
			break;
	}
	return TRUE;
	
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_Yx

//  输入参数:遥信序号,位寄存器标志
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;

===== 切片 112 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_Yx(WORD wBINo,BYTE bByteFalg)

===== 切片 113 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
{
	WORD wStartAddr;
	WORD wByteOffset;
	WORD wNo = 0;
	WORD wValue;
	BYTE bByteNum;
	BYTE bBitNo;
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bBIValue;
	DWORD dwNo;
	TSPIParaExInfo TYXParaExInfo;//单点遥信内容
	
	bByteNum = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount;	
	wStartAddr = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wStartAddr;
	TYXParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTSPIParaExInfo[wBINo];
	
	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始
	if(bByteFalg ==1 && TYXParaExInfo.ucDataType != YX_GET_BIT)//位寄存器的模式只解析按位取值的数值
		return FALSE;
	dwNo = (DWORD)wBINo;

===== 切片 114 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
switch( TYXParaExInfo.ucDataType)//遥信解释方式

===== 切片 115 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
{
		case YX_GET_BIT://按位取值
			if(bByteFalg ==1 )//位寄存器
			{
				wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)/8;
				bBitNo = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)%8;
				if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
					return FALSE;
			}else{
				if(TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit < 8)
				{
					wNo = 1;//低字节+1
					bBitNo = TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit%8;
				}else
					bBitNo = (TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit - 8)%8;
					
				wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2 + wNo;
				if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
					return FALSE;
			}
			bBIValue = ((pData[wByteOffset]>>bBitNo)&1)?PBI_ON:PBI_OFF;
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID,dwNo, bBIValue);
			break;
		case YX_GET_HBYTE_VALUE://高字节数值代表遥信序号
			wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2;
			if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
					return FALSE;
			WriteBI_CompareRxdNo(TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit,(WORD)pData[wByteOffset],dwNo);
			break;
		case YX_GET_LBYTE_VALUE://低字节数值代表遥信序号
			wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2 + 1;
			if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
					return FALSE;	
			WriteBI_CompareRxdNo(TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit,(WORD)pData[wByteOffset],dwNo);
			break;
		case YX_GET_WORD_VALUE://双字节（H_L）代表遥信序号（1个寄存器）
			wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2;
			if(wByteOffset + 2 > bByteNum)
				return FALSE;
			
			pData = pData + wByteOffset;
			wNo = MAKEWORD(pData[1],pData[0]);
			
			WriteBI_CompareRxdNo(TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit,wNo,dwNo);
			break;

===== 切片 116 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
case YX_GET_DBIT_10CLOSE://两个bit,双点遥信(10代表合)
			if(GetRegValue_FromStartAddr(TYXParaExInfo,&wValue) == FALSE)
				return FALSE;
			
			if(((wValue>>TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit)&0x03) ==2)
				bBIValue = PBI_ON;
			else
				bBIValue = PBI_OFF;
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID, dwNo, bBIValue);
			break;
		case YX_GET_DBIT_01CLOSE://两个bit,双点遥信(01代表合)
		
			if(GetRegValue_FromStartAddr(TYXParaExInfo,&wValue) == FALSE)
				return FALSE;
			if(((wValue>>TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit)&0x03) == 1)
				bBIValue = PBI_ON;
			else
				bBIValue = PBI_OFF;
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID, dwNo, bBIValue);
			break;
		default:
			return FALSE;	
	}

}

===== 切片 124 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
case YC_4INT_BCD://四字节BCD方式
			if(wByteOffset + 4 > bByteNum)
				return FALSE;
			
			pData = pData + wByteOffset;
			bVale[0] = Bcd2Byte(pData[0]);
			bVale[1] = Bcd2Byte(pData[1]);
			bVale[2] = Bcd2Byte(pData[2]);
			bVale[3] = Bcd2Byte(pData[3]);
			fAIValue = (float)(1000000*bVale[0] + 10000*bVale[1] + 100*bVale[2]  + bVale[3]*1);
		case YC_8I64_LH://8字节遥测ui64转float,低位在前L->H

			fAIValue = MakeToFloat_UI64(uAIValue,TAIParaExInfo.ucNegative);
			break;

			pTmp[2]=pData[2];
			pTmp[3]=pData[3];
			pTmp[4]=pData[4];
			pTmp[5]=pData[5];
			pTmp[6]=pData[6];
			pTmp[7]=pData[7];
			SwapFloatValue(pTmp,8);
			bAItFlag = 1;
			break;

case YC_8INT_2BLH_T1:// 8位=双字节整形遥测+年月日时分秒
			if(wByteOffset + 8 > bByteNum)
				return FALSE;
			
			pData = pData + wByteOffset;
	
			wAIValue = MAKEWORD(pData[0],pData[1]);
			fAIValue = MakeToFloat_WORD(wAIValue,TAIParaExInfo.ucNegative);
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Year = (pData[2] & 0x7F) + 2000;
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Month = (pData[3] & 0x0F);
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Day = (pData[4] & 0x1F);
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Hour = (pData[5] & 0x1F);
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Minute = (pData[6] & 0x3F);
			sysTimeAI.stTime.Second = (pData[7] & 0x3F);
			sysTimeAI.nNo = wAINo;
			sysTimeAI.bStatus = 0;
			bAItFlag = 2;
			break;	
		default:
			return FALSE;
	}

if(wAINo >= GetAINum(m_dwDevID))
		return FALSE ;
	
	if(bAItFlag == 1)
		fAIValue = dAIValue*(TAIParaExInfo.fScale);
	else
		fAIValue = fAIValue*(TAIParaExInfo.fScale);
	
	if(bAItFlag == 2)

		sysTimeAI.sValue = fAIValue;
		WriteSysTimeAit(m_dwDevID, &sysTimeAI);

内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_BOData(void)
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
	{	
		SetBOReturn(PBORET_FAIL);
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	TBO	*pBO;
	pBO=pGetBO();
	if(!pBO)
		return FALSE;
	if(pBO->dwCommand == TP_YKOPERATE || pBO->dwCommand == TP_YKDIRECT)
	{
		CheckClearSFlag(SF_AOEXESUCCESS);//来新的清除旧标志,重新开始计数查询
		SetSFlag(SF_BOEXESUCCESS);
		//m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex = 0;
	}	
		
	SetBOReturn(PBORET_SUCCESS);
	return TRUE;
}
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_AOData(void)
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
	{	
		SetAOReturn(PBORET_FAIL);
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	TAO	*pAO;
	pAO=pGetAO();
	if(!pAO)
		return FALSE;
	if(pAO->dwCommand == TP_AOOPERATE || pAO->dwCommand == TP_AODIRECT)
	{
		CheckClearSFlag(SF_BOEXESUCCESS);//清除旧标志重新开始
		SetSFlag(SF_AOEXESUCCESS);
		//m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex = 0;
	}
	
	SetAOReturn(PBORET_SUCCESS);
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: analyseOnlySOEContent
//  函数功能: 读取一个事件
//  输入参数：无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 135 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::AnalyseOnlySOEContent(PBYTE pSoeBuf)
{
	BYTE bCount,bSOENum;
	BYTE bStatus;
	DWORD dwTime;
	WORD wSecMsec;
	TSysTime Time;
	TSysTimeSoe SysTimeSoe;
	DWORD dwSoeAddr;
	DWORD dwBINo;
	
	ReadSysTime(&Time);
	
	if(!GetSOE_AddrStatusTime_FromBufdata(pSoeBuf,dwSoeAddr,bStatus,Time))
	{	
		printf("error:SoeAddr=%d(%04XH),bStatus=%d,YEAR=% d,MONTH=% d,DATE=% d,HOUR=% d,MIN=% d,SEC=% d,wSEC=% d\n",dwSoeAddr,dwSoeAddr ,bStatus,Time.Year, Time.Month, Time.Day, Time.Hour, Time.Minute, Time.Second, Time.MSecond);
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	printf("SoeAddr=%d(%04XH),bStatus=%d,YEAR=% d,MONTH=% d,DATE=% d,HOUR=% d,MIN=% d,SEC=% d,wSEC=% d \n",dwSoeAddr,dwSoeAddr ,bStatus,Time.Year, Time.Month, Time.Day, Time.Hour, Time.Minute, Time.Second, Time.MSecond );
	dwBINo = GetBINO_FromInfAddr(m_dwDevID,dwSoeAddr) ; 
	if(dwBINo == 0xFFFFFFFF)//未找到,继续下一个SOE
	{
		printf("not found!");
		return FALSE;
	}
	printf("found! bino=%d\n",dwBINo);
	SysTimeSoe.nNo = (WORD)dwBINo ;
	SysTimeSoe.bStatus = bStatus ;
	SysTimeSoe.stTime = Time ;
	WriteSysTimeSoe(m_dwDevID, &SysTimeSoe) ;	
	if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEConMod & MODBIT_0)       //需要写遥信状态
	  WriteBI(m_dwDevID,dwBINo,bStatus) ;
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 136 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
float CMModbEh::MakeToFloat_BYTE(BYTE data,BYTE negtype)
{
	short sAIValue;
	
	if(negtype == YCNEG_BUMA && (data&MODBIT_7))
		sAIValue = (short)data|0xff00;
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BITH && (data&MODBIT_7))
		sAIValue = 0 - (data & MODHBITNUM_7);
	else
		sAIValue = (short)data;
	
	return (float)sAIValue;
	
}
float CMModbEh::MakeToFloat_WORD(WORD data,BYTE negtype)
{
	short sAIValue;
	float fAIValue;
			
	if(negtype == YCNEG_BUMA && (data&MODBIT_15))
	{
		sAIValue = (short)data;
		fAIValue = (float)sAIValue;	
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BITH && (data&MODBIT_15))
	{
		sAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_15);
		fAIValue = (float)sAIValue;
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BIT12)
	{
		if(data&MODBIT_12)
			sAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_12);
		else
			sAIValue = data&MODHBITNUM_12;
		fAIValue = (float)sAIValue;
	}
	else
		fAIValue = (float)data;	
	
	return fAIValue;
	
}
float CMModbEh::MakeToFloat_DWORD(DWORD data,BYTE negtype)
{
	int iAIValue;
	float fAIValue;
			
	if(negtype == YCNEG_BUMA && (data&MODBIT_31))
	{
		iAIValue = (int)data;
		fAIValue = (float)iAIValue;	
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BITH && (data&MODBIT_31))
	{
		iAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_31);
		fAIValue = (float)iAIValue;
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BIT12)
	{
		if(data&MODBIT_12)
			iAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_12);
		else
			iAIValue = data&MODHBITNUM_12;
		fAIValue = (float)iAIValue;
	}
	else
		fAIValue = (float)data;	
	
	return fAIValue;
	
}

===== 切片 137 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
float CMModbEh::MakeToFloat_UI64(UI64 data,BYTE negtype)
{
	I64 IAIValue;
	float fAIValue;
			
	if(negtype == YCNEG_BUMA && (data&MODBIT_63))
	{
		IAIValue = (I64)data;
		fAIValue = (float)IAIValue;	
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BITH && (data&MODBIT_63))
	{
		IAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_63);
		fAIValue = (float)IAIValue;
	}
	else if(negtype == YCNEG_BIT12)
	{
		if(data&MODBIT_12)
			IAIValue = 0 - (data&MODHBITNUM_12);
		else
			IAIValue = data&MODHBITNUM_12;
		fAIValue = (float)IAIValue;
	}
	else
		fAIValue = (float)data;	
	
	return fAIValue;
	
}

void CMModbEh::WriteBI_CompareRxdNo(WORD wSetNo,WORD wRxdNo,DWORD dwBINo)
{
	if(wSetNo == wRxdNo)//是合位遥信
	{
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID, dwBINo, PBI_ON);		
	}else{//不是合位遥信
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID, dwBINo, PBI_OFF);
	}
	return;
}
void CMModbEh::WriteBIAndSOE_ALLOFF( TSysTime *time)
{
	TSysTimeSoe SysTimeSoe;
	
	for(DWORD dwBINo = 0;dwBINo < gpDevice[m_dwDevID].dwBINum; dwBINo++)
	{
		if(ReadBI(m_dwDevID,dwBINo) == PBI_ON)//如果合位全部变分				
		{
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID,dwBINo,PBI_OFF);
			if(time == NULL)
				continue;
			SysTimeSoe.nNo = (WORD)dwBINo;
			SysTimeSoe.bStatus = PBI_OFF;
			SysTimeSoe.stTime = *time;					
			WriteSysTimeSoe(m_dwDevID, &SysTimeSoe);
		}
	}
	return;
}

===== 切片 138 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::GetRegValue_FromStartAddr(TSPIParaExInfo TYXParaExInfo,WORD *wValue)
{
	WORD wStartAddr;
	WORD wByteOffset;
	BYTE bByteNum;
	PBYTE pData;
	
	bByteNum = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount;	
	wStartAddr = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wStartAddr;
	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始

	if(TYXParaExInfo.wRegisterBit < 8)//低字节
	{
		wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2 + 1;
		if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
			return FALSE;
		*wValue = (WORD)pData[wByteOffset];	
	}else{//高字节
		wByteOffset = (TYXParaExInfo.wStartAddr - wStartAddr)*2;
		if(wByteOffset >= bByteNum)
			return FALSE;
		*wValue = (WORD)(pData[wByteOffset]<<8);	//低字节不用自动填0
	}
	return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 实现SOE接受处理
//  输入参数:
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SOEReply(void)
{
	switch(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEType)
	{
		case SOEType_ContentQuery:
		    return Rxd_ContentQuery_SoeReply();
		case SOEType_First_GetNum:
		    return Rxd_GetNumFirst_SoeReply();
		case SOEType_First_GetNumPtr:
		    return Rxd_GetNumPtrFirst_SoeReply();
		case SOEType_HB2000XDL_02OPEN:
		    return Rxd_HB2000XDL02Open_SoeReply();
		case SOEType_SNDSOE_Query:
			return Rxd_SND_SoeReply();
		case SOEType_First_GetNumCalcPtr:
			return Rxd_GetNumCalcPtrFirst_SoeReply();		
		default:
		    return FALSE;
	}
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_ContentQuery_SoeReply
//  函数功能: 实现轮询SOE内容方式的SOE接收处理
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE失败
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 150 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_ContentQuery_SoeReply(void)
{
   	TModbEhSoeStepRec *pSoeStepRec;

	pSoeStepRec = m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].pSoeStepRec;
	switch(pSoeStepRec->wRxdSoeStep)
	{
		case 0:
			if(Rxd_ParseSoeContent())
			{
				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucRstSoeIndex > 0)
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;
			}
			break;
		case 1://复位SOE
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;	
			break;
		default:
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
		   	break;

	}
	if(pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep == 0)//本轮SOE轮询结束
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_CALLSOE);
		m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex++; //移到下一组
	}
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_GetNumFirst_SoeReply
//  函数功能: 实现先查询SOE个数方式的SOE接收处理
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE失败
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 151 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_GetNumFirst_SoeReply(void)
{
   	TModbEhSoeStepRec *pSoeStepRec;

	pSoeStepRec = m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].pSoeStepRec;
	switch(pSoeStepRec->wRxdSoeStep)
	{
		case 0://获取SOE的数量
			if(Rxd_SOENum())//SOE的数量不为0时，继续下一步查询内容
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;	
			break;
		case 1://获取SOE内容
			if(Rxd_ParseSoeContent())
			{
				m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum -= m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum;//还有未读的SOE数量
				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucRstSoeIndex > 0)
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 2;
				else if (m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//不需要复位SOE时,SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志
				{
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
					return TRUE;
				}	
			}else
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			break;
		case 2://复位SOE
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志
				return TRUE;	
			break;
		default:
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
		   	break;

	}
	if(pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep == 0)//SOE读取完毕，进行下一轮询组
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_CALLSOE);
		m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex++; //移到下一组
	}
	return TRUE;
}

bool CMModbEh::Rxd_GetNumPtrFirst_SoeReply(void)
{
   	TModbEhSoeStepRec *pSoeStepRec;

	pSoeStepRec = m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].pSoeStepRec;
	switch(pSoeStepRec->wRxdSoeStep)
	{
		case 0://获取SOE的数量与读指针
			if(Rxd_SOENumAndReadptr())//SOE的数量不为0时，继续下一步查询内容
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;	
			break;
		case 1://获取SOE内容
			if(Rxd_ParseSoeContent())
			{
				m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum -= m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum;//还有未读的SOE数量
				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucRstSoeIndex > 0)
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 2;
				else if (m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//不需要复位SOE时,SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志,
				{
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
					return TRUE;
				}	
			}else
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			break;
		case 2://复位SOE
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志
				return TRUE;	
			break;
		default:
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
		   	break;

	}
	if(pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep == 0)//SOE读取完毕，进行下一轮询组
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_CALLSOE);
		m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex++; //移到下一组
	}
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_ParseSoeContent
//  函数功能: 实现轮询SOE内容方式的SOE内容解析
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE失败
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 153 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_ParseSoeContent(void)
{
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bCount,bSOENum,bCountOK=0;
	BYTE bStatus;
	TSysTime Time;
	TSysTimeSoe SysTimeSoe;
	DWORD dwSoeAddr;
	DWORD dwBINo;
	
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	
	bCount = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount; 
	if(bCount < m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum)
		return FALSE;
	bSOENum = bCount/m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;
	m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum = bSOENum;

	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);
	ReadSysTime(&Time);
	for(int i = 0;i < bSOENum;i++)
	{
		if(!GetSOE_AddrStatusTime_FromBufdata(pData,dwSoeAddr,bStatus,Time))
			return FALSE;
		pData = pData + m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;

		dwBINo = GetBINO_FromInfAddr(m_dwDevID,dwSoeAddr);
		if(dwBINo == 0xFFFFFFFF)//未找到,继续下一个SOE
			continue;
		
		SysTimeSoe.nNo = (WORD)dwBINo;
		SysTimeSoe.bStatus = bStatus;
		SysTimeSoe.stTime = Time;
		WriteSysTimeSoe(m_dwDevID, &SysTimeSoe);
		if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEConMod & MODBIT_0)//需要写遥信状态
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID,dwBINo,bStatus);

		bCountOK++;
	}


	if(bCountOK==0)
		return FALSE;

	m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum = bCountOK;

	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_SOENum
//  函数功能: 获取装置内现有SOE的个数
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE失败
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 154 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SOENum(void)
{
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bCount;

	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	
	bCount = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount;
	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);
	if(bCount < 2)
	    return FALSE;
	m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum = MAKEWORD(pData[1],pData[0]);

    if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum <= 0)
	    return FALSE;
          
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_SOENumAndReadptr
//  函数功能: 获取装置内现有SOE的个数与读指针
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;FALSE失败
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SOENumAndReadptr(void)
{
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bCount;

	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	
	bCount = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount;
	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);
	if(bCount != 4)//长度固定
		return FALSE;
	m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum = MAKEWORD(pData[1],pData[0]);
	m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEPtr = MAKEWORD(pData[3],pData[2]);
	
   if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum <= 0)
	    return FALSE;
          
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_HB2000XDL02Open_SoeReply
//  函数功能: 实现HB-2000J小电流接地选线方式的SOE接收处理
//  输入参数:无

===== 切片 155 =====

bool CMModbEh::Rxd_HB2000XDL02Open_SoeReply(void)
{
   	TModbEhSoeStepRec *pSoeStepRec;

	pSoeStepRec = m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].pSoeStepRec;
	switch(pSoeStepRec->wRxdSoeStep)
	{
		case 0:
			if(Rxd_HB2000XDL02Open1_ParseSoe() == FALSE)
			    return FALSE;
			if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucRstSoeIndex > 0)
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;
			break;
		case 1://复位SOE
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;	
			break;
		default:
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
		   	break;

	}
	if(pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep == 0)//本轮SOE轮询结束
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_CALLSOE);
		m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex++; //移到下一组
	}
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 156 =====

bool CMModbEh::Rxd_HB2000XDL02Open1_ParseSoe(void)
{
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bCount,bSOENum;
	BYTE bStatus;
	TSysTime Time;
	TSysTimeSoe SysTimeSoe;
	DWORD dwSoeAddr;
	DWORD dwBINo;
	
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	
	bCount = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount; 
	if(bCount < m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum)
		return FALSE;
	bSOENum = bCount/m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;	
	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);
	ReadSysTime(&Time);
	for(int i = 0;i < bSOENum;i++)
	{
		if(!GetSOE_AddrStatusTime_FromBufdata(pData,dwSoeAddr,bStatus,Time))
			return FALSE;
		pData = pData + m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;
		if(m_wSOECode != 1 && m_wSOECode != 3)//接地恢复
		{
			WriteBIAndSOE_ALLOFF(&Time);
			return TRUE;
		}
		dwBINo = GetBINO_FromInfAddr(m_dwDevID,dwSoeAddr);
		if(dwBINo == 0xFFFFFFFF)//未找到,继续下一个SOE
			continue;
		
		SysTimeSoe.nNo = (WORD)dwBINo;
		SysTimeSoe.bStatus = bStatus;
		SysTimeSoe.stTime = Time;
		WriteSysTimeSoe(m_dwDevID, &SysTimeSoe);
		if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEConMod & MODBIT_0)//需要写遥信状态
			WriteBI(m_dwDevID,dwBINo,bStatus);
	}
	return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_GetNumCalcPtrFirst_SoeReply
//  函数功能: 实现先查询SOE个数并计算指针方式的SOE接收处理
//  输入参数:无
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 166 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_GetNumCalcPtrFirst_SoeReply(void)
{
   	TModbEhSoeStepRec *pSoeStepRec;

	pSoeStepRec = m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].pSoeStepRec;
	switch(pSoeStepRec->wRxdSoeStep)
	{
		case 0://获取SOE的数量
			if(Rxd_SOENum())//SOE的数量不为0时，继续下一步查询内容
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;	
			break;
		case 1://获取SOE内容
			if(Rxd_ParseSoeContent())
			{
				m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum -= m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum;//还有未读的SOE数量
				m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEPtr += m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wRecSOENum;
				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucRstSoeIndex > 0)
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 2;
				else if (m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//不需要复位SOE时,SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志
				{
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 1;
					return TRUE;
				}
				else if (m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum == 0)//DMP300需要再往后问一个用以通知从站，不同厂家这里可能要求不同
				{
					pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 3;
					return TRUE;
				}
			}else
				pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			break;
		case 2://复位SOE
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
			if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOENum > 0)//SOE未读完开始新的一轮SOE读取，不清SOE标志
				return TRUE;	
			break;
		default:
			pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep = 0;
		   	break;

	}
	if(pSoeStepRec->wTxdSoeStep == 0)//SOE读取完毕，进行下一轮询组
	{
		ClearSFlag(SF_CALLSOE);
		m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex++; //移到下一组
	}
	return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Rxd_ALLSND_SOE
//  函数功能: 实现snd所有SOE接收处理
//  输入参数:
//  返回值:    TRUE,成功;
//			FALSE 召唤结束

===== 切片 167 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_ALLSND_SOE(void)
{   
	PBYTE pData;
	BYTE bCount,bSOENum;
	BYTE bStatus;
	DWORD dwTime;
	WORD wSecMsec;
	TSysTime Time;
	TSysTimeSoe SysTimeSoe;
	DWORD dwSoeAddr;
	DWORD dwBINo;
	DWORD wCurrentSerialNUM;
	bool isFinished = FALSE;
	

	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	
	bCount = m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucByteCount; 
	if(bCount < m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum)
		return FALSE;
	bSOENum = bCount/m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;	

	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData); //指向第一个事件开始
	pData += m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum * (bSOENum-1);//指向第10个事件开始

	m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wLastRecSerialNum = MAKEDWORD(pData[15],pData[14],pData[13],pData[12]);	
	for(int i=0; i<bSOENum; i++)
	{
		wCurrentSerialNUM = MAKEDWORD(pData[15],pData[14],pData[13],pData[12]);	 //比较最后soe序号与旧doe事件序号的关系
		//printf("oldnum %d , curret num %d\n",m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wOldSoeSerialNum,wCurrentSerialNUM);
		if(wCurrentSerialNUM <= m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wOldSoeSerialNum)//已经读过
			isFinished = TRUE;
		else
			AnalyseOnlySOEContent(pData);
		pData -= m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum;
	}
	if(isFinished)
	{
		m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wOldSoeSerialNum = m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wNewSoeSerialNum;
		return FALSE;
	}
   
  return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 实现施耐德MTZ系列录波文件接受处理

//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 169 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_FileReply(void)
{
	bool ret;
	
	switch(m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep)  
	{
		case SND_FS_Init:
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_Serial(0);
			break;
		case SND_FS_CallLastSerial://读最新序号，以检查是否有新的文件
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_Serial(1);
			break;
		case SND_FS_GetPath1_SetCmd:		//获取WFC路径第1步，设置寄存器，下发命令
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath1_Set();
			break;
		case SND_FS_GetPath2_ReadResult: 		//获取WFC路径第2步，读执行结果
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath2_Result();
			break;
		case SND_FS_GetPath3_ReadPath:			//获取WFC路径第3步，读路径
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath3_Path();
			break;
		case SND_FS_OpenFile1_SetCmd:			//打开文件第1步，下发命令
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_OpenFile1_Set();
			break;
		case SND_FS_OpenFile2_ReadResult:		//打开文件第1步，读取命令执行结果
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_OpenFile2_Result();
			break;
		case SND_FS_ReadFile1_SetCmd:			//读取文件第1步，设置命令
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_ReadFile1_Set();
			break;
		case SND_FS_ReadFile2_ReadContent:		//读取文件第2步，读取文件内容
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_ReadFile2_Content();
			break;
		case SND_FS_CloseFile1_SetCmd:			//关闭文件第1步，设置命令
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_CloseFile1_Set();
			break;
		case SND_FS_CloseFile2_ReadResult:		//关闭文件第2步，读取文件内容
			ret = Rxd_SNDMTZ_CloseFile2_Result();
			break;
		default:
			break;
	}
	if(ret)
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucRetry = 0;
	return ret;	
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 接收最新序列号
//  输入参数: CheckFlag:不是第一次，需要判断序号是否有改变
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;

===== 切片 170 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_Serial(BYTE CheckFlag)
{
	WORD wSerial;
	PBYTE pData;
	
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;

	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;

	pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始
	
	wSerial = MAKEWORD(pData[3],pData[2]);
	
	
	if(wSerial>254)
	{
		printf("->%s:error:rec serial=%d!\n",pDevDspName,wSerial);
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	if(m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucLastSerial != wSerial)//有新录波
	{
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_GetPath1_SetCmd;
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucReadingSerial = wSerial;
		printf("->%s:rec serial=%d,have new! ,last=%d\n",pDevDspName,wSerial,m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucLastSerial);
	}
	else
	{
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_End;
		printf("->%s:rec serial=%d,same!\n",pDevDspName,wSerial);
	}

	if(m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucLastSerial == 0)
		SNDMTZ_WriteFileSerialNo(wSerial);
	return TRUE;
	
}

===== 切片 171 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath1_Set(void)
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	
	printf("->%s:rec get path cmd OK\n",pDevDspName);
	m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_GetPath2_ReadResult; //下一步读寄存器
	return TRUE;
}
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 获取WFC路径第2步，读寄存器后的响应
//  输入参数: 
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath2_Result()
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;

	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	TSNDMTZCmdResult *pResult = (TSNDMTZCmdResult*)&(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始，8019寄存器内容
	WORD wRetCmd = MAKEWORD(pResult->Cmd_L,pResult->Cmd_H);
	WORD wResult = MAKEWORD(pResult->Result_L,pResult->Result_H);
	WORD wLastLen = MAKEWORD(pResult->LastLen_L,pResult->LastLen_H);

	if(wRetCmd != SND_CMD_GET_PATH || wResult != 0 || wLastLen == 0)
	{
		printf("->%s:rec result for get path error! cmd=%d,result=%04XH,LastLen=%d\n",pDevDspName,wRetCmd,wResult,wLastLen);
		return FALSE;
	}
	printf("->%s:rec result for get path OK!\n",pDevDspName);
	m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_GetPath3_ReadPath;
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 172 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 获取WFC路径第3步，读路径的返回
//  输入参数: 
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_GetPath3_Path()
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;

	PBYTE pData = &(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始 8022寄存器
	TSNDMTZFile *pFileRec = &m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo];
	WORD wOffset;

	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	
	if(pData[0] != 0 || pData[1] != 1)
	{
		printf("->%s:rec path error!\n");
		return FALSE;
	}
	
	wOffset = (8025 - 8022)*2; //简单WFC文件路径开始
	strncpy(pFileRec->SimplePath,(char*)(pData+wOffset),sizeof(pFileRec->SimplePath)-1);

	wOffset = (8050 - 8022)*2; //高级WFC文件路径开始
	strncpy(pFileRec->AdvancedPath,(char*)(pData+wOffset),sizeof(pFileRec->AdvancedPath)-1);
	
	ReadSysTime(&pFileRec->SaveTime);
	
	printf("->rec path OK, TCI=%s, ADV=%s\n",pFileRec->SimplePath,pFileRec->AdvancedPath);
	pFileRec->eReadStep = SND_FS_OpenFile1_SetCmd;
	return TRUE;
}

		return FALSE;
	}	
	printf("-%s:>rec open result OK, fileID=%d(%02XH),save=%s\n",pDevDspName,pFileRec->ucFileID,pFileRec->ucFileID,pFileRec->SaveFileName);
	return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数功能: 读取文件第1步，设置寄存器后的响应
//  输入参数: 
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_ReadFile1_Set(void)
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;
	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	
	printf("->%s:rec read file cmd OK\n",pDevDspName);
	m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_ReadFile2_ReadContent; //下一步读文件内容
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 182 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Rxd_SNDMTZ_CloseFile2_Result()
{
	if(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucFunc >= 0x80)//否定回复
		return FALSE;

	char* pDevDspName = pGetDevice(m_dwDevID)->szParaName;
	if(pDevDspName == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	TSNDMTZCmdResult *pResult = (TSNDMTZCmdResult*)&(m_pRxdFm->RxdReadFm.ucData);//数据开始，8019寄存器内容
	TSNDMTZFile *pFileRec = &m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo];
	WORD wOffset;

	
	WORD wRetCmd = MAKEWORD(pResult->Cmd_L,pResult->Cmd_H);
	WORD wResult = MAKEWORD(pResult->Result_L,pResult->Result_H);
	WORD wLastLen = MAKEWORD(pResult->LastLen_L,pResult->LastLen_H);

	if(wRetCmd != SND_CMD_CLOSE_FILE || wResult != 0 || wLastLen != 0)
	{
		printf("->%s:rec result for close error! cmd=%d,result=%04XH,LastLen=%d\n",pDevDspName,wRetCmd,wResult,wLastLen);
		return FALSE;
	}
	printf("->%s:rec result for close OK,fileID=%d,type=%d\n",pDevDspName,pFileRec->ucFileID,pFileRec->ucFileType);

	if(pFileRec->ucFileType < 4) //召唤下一个文件
	{
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_OpenFile1_SetCmd;
		pFileRec->ucFileType ++;
		pFileRec->dwOffset = 0;
	}
	else //该序号召唤结束
	{
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].eReadStep = SND_FS_CallLastSerial;
		m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucLastSerial = m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucReadingSerial;
		SNDMTZ_WriteFileSerialNo(m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucLastSerial);
		printf("====%s:end to call WFC %d fileID=%d,type=%d\n",pDevDspName,m_pSNDFile[m_dwDevNo].ucReadingSerial);
	}
	return TRUE;
}

//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 183 =====
来源: mmodbehr.cpp
内容:
//  函数功能: 将最新的录波序列号写入文件保存
//  输入参数: 
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
void CMModbEh::SNDMTZ_WriteFileSerialNo(BYTE ucLastSerialNo)
{
	char sDevName[20];
	
	if(!(::GetDevParaName(m_dwDevID,sDevName)))
		return;
	
	bool rc = SetConfigItemInt("SNDMTZ.ini",sDevName,  ucLastSerialNo, "WFCSerial");
	if(rc)
		printf("%s:success to write ini file! no=%d\n",sDevName,ucLastSerialNo);
	else
		printf("%s:fail to write ini file! no=%d\n",sDevName,ucLastSerialNo);
}

//=========================================================================================================
//  函数功能: 编辑报文头
//  输入参数: 无
//  返回值：  成功则返回TRUE
//=========================================================================================================
bool CMModbEh::Txd_Head(BYTE nFunCode,WORD wStartAddr,WORD wNum)
{
	m_Txd.ReadPtr=0;
	m_Txd.WritePtr=0;	
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTransMode)
	{
		m_wNetcount++;
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(m_wNetcount); 	//事务处理标识符
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(m_wNetcount);					
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00; 	//协议标识符
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00;					
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00; 	//长度
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 06;					
	}
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = GetAddress(); //站号
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = nFunCode;	
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(wStartAddr);
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(wStartAddr);
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(wNum);
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(wNum);
	
	return TRUE;
}
//=========================================================================================================
//  函数功能: 编辑报文尾
//  输入参数: 无
//  返回值：  成功则返回TRUE
//=========================================================================================================

===== 切片 200 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Txd_Tail(void)
{
	WORD Rc;
	WORD wLen;
	
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTransMode )//网络模式
	{
		wLen = m_Txd.WritePtr-6;
		m_Txd.Buf[4] = HIBYTE(wLen); //长度
		m_Txd.Buf[5] = LOBYTE(wLen);			
	}
	else
	{
		if(m_ComCfg.ucCRCType == CRC16_XMODEM)		
			Rc=CRC16XMODEM((PBYTE)m_Txd.Buf,m_Txd.WritePtr);
		else
			Rc=CRC16MODBUS((PBYTE)m_Txd.Buf,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	
		if(m_ComCfg.ucTranSequence == FirstHIBYTE ) //高字节调整字节序以备发送
			Rc = SwapWord(Rc);	
	
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(Rc);
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(Rc);
	}
	if(m_ComCfg.wControlMode&MODBIT_0)
		SM4EncryptTxdFM();
	
	//	ResetRxdPtr();
	WriteToComm(GetAddress(),0);
	// StartCommEvent(MCC_CommTxdIdle,m_ComCfg.wTxdCommIdle);	
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 201 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::TxdQueryOneRound(void)
{
	WORD wNo;
	BYTE bTxdNum;
	TSendFunInfo *pTxdFunInfo;

	bTxdNum = m_pDevCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendNum;
	if(bTxdNum <= 0)
		return FALSE;
	
	for(wNo = m_pPollRec[m_dwDevNo].wIndex; wNo < bTxdNum; wNo ++)
	{
		pTxdFunInfo = &m_pDevCfg[m_dwDevNo].pSendFunInfo[wNo];//指向每一功能码的具体设置	
		switch (pTxdFunInfo->ucSendDatatype)
		{
			case Modbus_SendData_BASE:
				if(Txd_QueryData(pTxdFunInfo,wNo,Modbus_DataType_BASE,1))
					return TRUE;	
				break;
//			case Modbus_SendData_SOENum:
//				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEType == SOEType_First_GetNum || m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEType == SOEType_First_GetNumPtr)
//				{
//					if(Txd_QueryData(pTxdFunInfo,wNo,Modbus_DataType_SOENUM,1))
//						return TRUE;
//				}
//				break;
//			case Modbus_SendData_SOEConsent:
//				if(m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEType != SOEType_First_GetNum && m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].wSOEType != SOEType_First_GetNumPtr)
//				{
//					if(Txd_QueryData(pTxdFunInfo,wNo,Modbus_DataType_SOE,1))
//						return TRUE;
//				}
//				break;
			default:
				break;
		}
	}
	return FALSE;
}

===== 切片 202 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Txd_QueryData(TSendFunInfo *pTxdFunInfo,WORD index,WORD type,WORD wTxdFlag)
{
	if((pTxdFunInfo->wSendFlag&MODBIT_0)||wTxdFlag == 1)
	{
		Txd_Head(pTxdFunInfo->ucFunCode,pTxdFunInfo->wStartAddr, pTxdFunInfo->wRegisterNum);
		Txd_Tail();
		if(pTxdFunInfo->wSendInterval > 0)
		{
			pTxdFunInfo->wSendFlag &= ~MODBIT_0;
			pTxdFunInfo->wSendCount = pTxdFunInfo->wSendInterval;
		}	
		RecordPollRec(index+1, pTxdFunInfo->ucFunCode,pTxdFunInfo->wStartAddr,pTxdFunInfo->wRegisterNum,type,m_Txd.WritePtr);
		return TRUE;
	}
	return FALSE;
}

//	输入参数: 无

//*******************************************************************************************//

===== 切片 203 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Txd_YKCommand(void)
{
	DWORD dwBOIndex;
	BYTE bCentLen;
	BYTE *pCent;
	TBOParaExInfo  TBOParaExInfo;//遥控内容
	
	TBO* pBO=pGetBO();
	if(pBO == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	dwBOIndex = GetRealYkNo(m_dwDevID, pBO->dwNo, pBO->dwAttrib);
	if(dwBOIndex == 0xFFFFFFFF)
		return FALSE;
	if(dwBOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wBONum)
		return FALSE;
	TBOParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[dwBOIndex];
	switch(pBO->dwCommand)
	{
		case TP_YKSELECT:
			bCentLen = TBOParaExInfo.ucSelectNum;
			pCent = TBOParaExInfo.ucSelectCent;
			break;
		case TP_YKOPERATE:
		case TP_YKDIRECT:
			bCentLen = TBOParaExInfo.ucOperateNum;
			pCent = TBOParaExInfo.ucOperateCent;
			if(TBOParaExInfo.ucRestetFalg && TBOParaExInfo.ucCancelNum)
			{
				SetSFlag(SF_BOSetTime);
				SetTimer(TM_BORESET,TBOParaExInfo.wResetTime);
				RecordBOexeRec(dwBOIndex);
			}
			break;
		case TP_YKCANCEL:
			bCentLen = TBOParaExInfo.ucCancelNum;
			pCent = TBOParaExInfo.ucCancelCent;
			break;
		default:
			return FALSE;
	}
	if(bCentLen == 0)//没有设置
		return FALSE;
	
	Txd_OrderContent_Write(bCentLen,pCent);
	Txd_Tail();
	RecordPollRec(0, pCent[0],0,0,Modbus_DataType_BO,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	return TRUE;
}

bool CMModbEh::Txd_YKCommand_Time(DWORD addr)

	if(dwBOIndex == 0xFFFFFFFF)
		return FALSE;
	if(dwBOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wBONum)
		return FALSE;
	TBOParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[dwBOIndex];

	bCentLen = TBOParaExInfo.ucOperateNum;
	pCent = TBOParaExInfo.ucOperateCent;


	if(bCentLen == 0)//没有设置
		return FALSE;
	
	Txd_OrderContent_Write(bCentLen,pCent);
	Txd_Tail();
	//RecordPollRec(0, pCent[0],0,0,Modbus_DataType_BO,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	return TRUE;
}
bool CMModbEh::Txd_YKRESET(void)
{
	DWORD dwBOIndex;
	BYTE bCentLen;
	BYTE *pCent;
	
	dwBOIndex = m_pBOResetRec[m_dwDevNo].dwBOIndex;	
	if(dwBOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wBONum)
		return FALSE;
	bCentLen = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[dwBOIndex].ucCancelNum;
	pCent = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTBOParaExInfo[dwBOIndex].ucCancelCent;
	
	Txd_OrderContent_Write(bCentLen,pCent);
	Txd_Tail();
	RecordPollRec(0, pCent[0],0,0,Modbus_DataType_NULL,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	return TRUE;
}

//	函数功能: 发送遥调命令
//	输入参数: 无
//	返回值:	  TRUE,有报文发送; FALSE:错误或者只设置标志,无实际报文发送

===== 切片 205 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
bool CMModbEh::Txd_AOCommand(void)
{
	DWORD dwAOIndex;
	BYTE bCentLen;
	BYTE *pCent;
	TAOParaExInfo  TAOParaExInfo;//遥调内容
	
	TAO* pAO=pGetAO();
	if(pAO == NULL)
		return FALSE;
	dwAOIndex=GetAONo(m_dwDevID, pAO->wNo);
	if(dwAOIndex == 0xFFFFFFFF)
		return FALSE;
	
	if(dwAOIndex >= m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].wAONum)
		return FALSE;
	
	TAOParaExInfo = m_pRealParaCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTAOParaExInfo[dwAOIndex];
	switch(pAO->dwCommand)
	{
		case TP_AOSELECT:
			bCentLen = TAOParaExInfo.ucSelectNum;
			pCent = TAOParaExInfo.ucSelectCent;
			break;
		case TP_AOOPERATE:
		case TP_AODIRECT:
			bCentLen = TAOParaExInfo.ucOperateNum;
			pCent = TAOParaExInfo.ucOperateCent;
			break;
		default:
			return FALSE;
	}
	if(bCentLen == 0)//没有设置
		return FALSE;
	Txd_OrderContent_Write(bCentLen,pCent);
	if(Txd_AOValue(pAO->fAoValue,TAOParaExInfo.ucDataType,TAOParaExInfo.ucNegative) == FALSE)
		return FALSE;
	Txd_Tail();
	RecordPollRec(0,pCent[0],0,0,Modbus_DataType_AO,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	return TRUE;
}

===== 切片 214 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
//*******************************************************************************************//
//	函数名称: Txd_OrderContent_Write
//	函数功能: 发送内容(包含地址)
//	输入参数:len:控制命令自定义字符串长度,data：自定义字符串内容16进制
//	返回值:	  TRUE,成功;
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Txd_OrderContent_Write(BYTE len,BYTE *data)
{
	m_Txd.ReadPtr=0;
	m_Txd.WritePtr=0;
	if(m_ComCfg.ucTransMode)
	{
		m_wNetcount++;
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(m_wNetcount); 	//事务处理标识符
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(m_wNetcount);					
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00; 	//协议标识符
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00;					
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 00; 	//长度
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 06;					
	}
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = GetAddress(); //站号

	if(data != NULL)
	{
		for(int i=0;i<len;i++)
		{
			m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = data[i];
		}
	}
	return TRUE;
}

{
	BYTE bRow;		//行数，与维护软件Modbus规约任务级设置下的表格相对应
	BYTE bSendCnt;
	WORD wByteNum;	//字节数，用于计算寄存器个数
	WORD wRegNum;	//寄存器个数
	TSysTime RtuTime;
	
	wByteNum = 0;
	ReadSysTime((TSysTime *)&RtuTime);

	if(m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmFunCode == 43)//封装接口传输
		Txd_MEI_Head(m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmFunCode, m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].wTmStartAddr, m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTimeMode & MODBIT_0);
	else
		Txd_Write_Muti_Head(m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmFunCode, m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].wTmStartAddr, 0, m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTimeMode & MODBIT_0);

	/* 一次发送行数为0，即一次对时发送所有内容*/

===== 切片 217 =====
来源: mmodbeht.cpp
内容:
if (m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmRowNum == 0)
	{
		
		for (bRow = 0;bRow < m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmSendNum;bRow++)
		{
			/* 组帧失败（该行对时选项未实现或字节数为0）则不发送，避免发出错误的对时报文 */
			if (Txd_TimeInfoOneRow(bRow,RtuTime) == FALSE)
				return FALSE;
			wByteNum += m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTimeInfo[bRow].ucByteNum;
			m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendTimeCnt = 0;//已经全部发完	
		}	
	}
	else	/*一次发送行数不为0,一次对时发送多次对时报文*/
	{
		bSendCnt = m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendTimeCnt;
		for (bRow = 0;bRow < m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmRowNum;bRow++)
		{
			/* 组帧失败（该行对时选项未实现或字节数为0）则不发送，避免发出错误的对时报文 */
			if (Txd_TimeInfoOneRow(bSendCnt + bRow,RtuTime) == FALSE)
				return FALSE;
			wByteNum += m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].pTimeInfo[bSendCnt + bRow].ucByteNum;
		}
		m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendTimeCnt += m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmRowNum;
		
		if (m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendTimeCnt == m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmSendNum)
			m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSendTimeCnt = 0;//已经全部发完	
	}

	/* 如果发送的字节数之和为0，不发送报文 */
	if (wByteNum == 0)
		return FALSE;

	if(m_pTimeCfg[m_dwDevNo].ucTmFunCode != 43)//不是封装接口传输
	{
		wRegNum = (wByteNum + 1)/2;
		if (m_ComCfg.ucTransMode)	/*以太网Modbus*/
		{
			m_Txd.Buf[10] = HIBYTE(wRegNum);
			m_Txd.Buf[11] = LOBYTE(wRegNum);
			m_Txd.Buf[12] = (BYTE)wByteNum;
		}
		else
		{
			m_Txd.Buf[4] = HIBYTE(wRegNum);
			m_Txd.Buf[5] = LOBYTE(wRegNum);
			m_Txd.Buf[6] = (BYTE)wByteNum;
		}
	}
	Txd_Tail();

	return TRUE;
}

case Modbus_TimeSelect_DayFrom19991231:
		{
			TSysTime baseTime;
			DWORD dwBaseSec;
			DWORD dwCurSec;
			DWORD dwDays;
			DWORD dwSec;

			baseTime.Year = 1999;
			baseTime.Month = 12;
			baseTime.Day = 31;
			baseTime.Hour = 0;
			baseTime.Minute = 0;
			baseTime.Second = 0;
			baseTime.MSecond = 0;

			dwBaseSec = DostoUnix(&baseTime, 1999);
			dwCurSec = DostoUnix(&RtuTime, 1999);

			/* 系统时钟非法或早于基准日期时不发对时，否则会把装置时钟设成1999-12-31 */
			if ((dwBaseSec == 0) || (dwCurSec <= dwBaseSec))
			{
				m_Txd.WritePtr = wStartPtr;	/* 回退写指针，本行不占报文空间 */
				return FALSE;
			}
			dwDays = (dwCurSec - dwBaseSec) / 86400;
			dwSec = dwCurSec - dwBaseSec;

			if (pTimeInfo->ucByteSequence == Modbus_Time_Little_Endian)
			{
				if (pTimeInfo->ucByteNum == 1)
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
				else
				{
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr-1] = LOBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (pTimeInfo->ucByteNum == 1)
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
				else
				{
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr-1] = HIBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
					m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE((WORD)dwDays);
				}
			}	
			
		}
			break;

case Modbus_TimeSelect_SecFrom19991231://基准日期 1999-12-31 起算的总秒数
			{
				TSysTime baseTime;
				DWORD dwBaseSec, dwCurSec, dwSec;
				BYTE  bySec[4];
			
				baseTime.Year = 1999;  baseTime.Month = 12;  baseTime.Day = 31;
				baseTime.Hour = 0;     baseTime.Minute = 0;  baseTime.Second = 0;
				baseTime.MSecond = 0;
			
				dwBaseSec = DostoUnix(&baseTime, 1999);
				dwCurSec  = DostoUnix(&RtuTime, 1999);

				/* 系统时钟非法或早于基准日期时不发对时，否则会把装置时钟设成1999-12-31 */
				if ((dwBaseSec == 0) || (dwCurSec <= dwBaseSec))
				{
					m_Txd.WritePtr = wStartPtr;	/* 回退写指针，本行不占报文空间 */
					return FALSE;
				}
				dwSec = dwCurSec - dwBaseSec;
			
				/* 本字段字序固定低字在前: 低字高字节,低字低字节,高字高字节,高字低字节 */
				bySec[0] = LHBYTE(dwSec);
				bySec[1] = LLBYTE(dwSec);
				bySec[2] = HHBYTE(dwSec);
				bySec[3] = HLBYTE(dwSec);
				if (pTimeInfo->ucByteSequence == Modbus_Time_Little_Endian)/* 低字节在前：整体倒序 */
				{
					bySec[0] = HLBYTE(dwSec);
					bySec[1] = HHBYTE(dwSec);
					bySec[2] = LLBYTE(dwSec);
					bySec[3] = LHBYTE(dwSec);
				}
			
				if (pTimeInfo->ucByteNum > 4)
					memcpy(&m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr - 3], bySec, 4);
				else
					memcpy(&m_Txd.Buf[wStartPtr], bySec, pTimeInfo->ucByteNum);
			
				m_Txd.WritePtr++;
			}
				break;
		/* 下列对时内容至少需要8字节才能完整表示 */

//*******************************************************************************************//
//  函数名称: Txd_GetAllSNDSoe
//  函数功能: 读取所有soe
//  输入参数:空
//  返回值:   TRUE,成功;
//  李艳萍
//*******************************************************************************************//
bool CMModbEh::Txd_GetAllSNDSoe(void)
{
    WORD wRegisterNum=0;
	TSendFunInfo *pTxdFunInfo;
	WORD wSoeNum;
	wSoeNum=m_sndJYSoe[m_dwDevNo].wSndSoeNum;	//8032 Num
	wRegisterNum = wSoeNum * (m_pSOECfg[m_dwDevNo].ucSoeByteNum / 2);	 //	寄存器个数=soe个数*soe长度
	Txd_Head( SND_SOE_WFUN , RD_SOE_SJ_SOEFIRSTADD , wRegisterNum );
	Txd_Tail();
	//RecordPollRec(0, pTxdFunInfo->ucFunCode,pTxdFunInfo->wStartAddr,wRegisterNum,Modbus_DataType_SOE,m_Txd.WritePtr);
	return TRUE;
}

bool CMModbEh::Txd_WRmulHold(WORD wFuncCode , WORD wStartAddr, WORD wNum, WORD wByteNum, WORD wSer8006,WORD wRequstvalue, DWORD wSerialNum)
{
	Txd_Head(wFuncCode,wStartAddr,wNum);
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = wByteNum;
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_MLMVALUE);//写入8000
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_MLMVALUE);//写入8000
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_BYTEVALUE);//写入8001
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_BYTEVALUE);//写入8001
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_MDVALUE1);//写入8002  8705/5377
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(WT_SOE_SJ_MDVALUE1);//写入8002
	for(int i = 0;i<6;i++) //8003-8005
	{
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 0;
	}
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(wSer8006);//写入8006
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(wSer8006);//写入8006
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HIBYTE(wRequstvalue);//写入8007
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LOBYTE(wRequstvalue);//写入8007
	for (int i = 0;i<8;i++)  //8008-8011
	{
		m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 0;
	}
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HHBYTE(wSerialNum);//写入8012
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = HLBYTE(wSerialNum);//写入8012
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LHBYTE(wSerialNum);//写入8013
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = LLBYTE(wSerialNum);//写入8013
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 0x07;//写入8014 位8、9、10置1
	m_Txd.Buf[m_Txd.WritePtr++] = 0x00;//写入8014 位8、9、10置1
	Txd_Tail();	
	return TRUE;
}
